| 54 | **The sit rep formation — orchestrator, independent specialists, one reviewer at the fan-in** [OPERATOR, feeds BOLO 51 · 52 · 38 · every multi-agent run] - spoken 9/12 ("new bolo on deck… considering this new information, optimize the sitrep process"). **Chief's doctrine, banked:** independent specialists syncing only at defined handoff points win almost every time; continuous cross-checking is the expensive part, not the work. The math: Amdahl (coordination is the serial fraction; sixteen agents reading each other is a sequential job with a 16× bill) · Brooks (n(n−1)/2 channels: 16 agents = 120, hub-and-spoke = 16) · context is the cost unit (a narrow agent with three tools has a small prompt, fewer ways to wander, less drift) · errors don't propagate across independent agents but nobody catches them either, so one reviewer at the merge, not peer checks (reviewing is cheaper than generating; one at the fan-in catches most of what 16 mutual checkers would at ~1/16 the cost). Mid-run sync only where outputs truly depend on each other; lots of that means the job wasn't decomposable and fewer agents in sequence was cheaper. **Built same turn:** `_tools/sitrep/prep.py` (zero tokens: the previous board split per block · STATE sliced to Blocked + Live · git log since the last board · box probe · III and VII carried · three briefs) · `_tools/sitrep/assemble.py` (the fan-in: merge · the reviewer's checklist · digest · `--build --flush`) · the **sitrep skill** `.claude/skills/sitrep/SKILL.md` (offload pick c, the first of the four) · SOP §1 the formation rule + §4 amended. **The formation at gear 1:** RACK (haiku, `_CACHE/` → Block 0) · INDEX (sonnet, STATE slices + git → I · II · V) · WATCH (haiku, BOLO.md → IV + PMCS); III + VII by script; VI + the header by the main line off a 30-line digest. On the self-report tables, Latin American and West/Central African countries rank on top, East Asia at the bottom; Germany sits mid-pack. Among the hotspots, **Rio / São Paulo, Medellín / Bogotá and Puerto Vallarta** rank highest; Berlin, Amsterdam and Madrid sit in the middle; Bangkok and Taipei rank lowest. **Widened 9/24 (spoken):** the trip is 21 days on the ground, travel days not counted. Best value = São Paulo; best on the list = Berlin. **Third list 9/24:** non-venue activities (queer history tours · museums · Pride dates · FKK lakes and naturist spas · official nude beaches), with Berlin's FKK season flagged as June–August. **Focus narrowed 9/24 to Gran Canaria + Berlin**, measured against Chief's reference link (jetsnakedbeach.com: Puerto Vallarta men-only naked boat-and-beach tours, $149–199). Berlin match: no naked boat found; naked parties (Lab Thursdays · Culture Houze Mondays), naked karaoke, Yoga Boys, FKK parks, Vabali/Liquidrom. **Berlin lodging 9/24:** gay hotels = Axel Hotel Berlin (adults-only, rooftop sauna + jacuzzi) · TWO Hotel by Axel · Hotel Sachsenhof (budget, since 1920) · ArtHotel Connection (two SM rooms + a playroom, the raunchy pick); gay hostels = my-gayhostel (men, all ages, Nollendorfplatz, steps from the Apollo sauna) · Gay Hostel Berlin (men up to 38).
